Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The computations operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 971. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 856 with 1 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_971(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 971. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^856(S, F) is trivial. QED.
